# Garage by track

These tables are what an empty garage draft looks like on the 10 base tracks: every module ranked on its own, before anyone has picked. A live suggestion still depends on what is on offer and what you have already taken. Lower rank is better; 1 is best.

<p class="callout"><strong>These ranks may be newer than the in-game PitBoss panel.</strong> They come from HeatAI’s Oct 24 rankings, which have not shipped inside PitBoss yet. Until that vendor file is in the extension, the panel can show an older order than the tables on this page.</p>

**Methodology:** For each of the 10 base BGA Heat tracks, every one of the 65 garage modules was raced alone in 500 simulated 4-car races by the PitBoss AI; modules are ranked best→worst by average finishing position (lower is better).

Modules that share a name are different cards. The module column shows that card's face; the tooltip and screen-reader name use the PitBoss label, such as “4 wheel drive (0, boost)”. Speed uses the PitBoss card glyph: a number, a range such as 1-5, or + / ++ / +++ when the printed speed is 0. Details shows the HUD symbols.

Module faces and those symbols are the same Board Game Arena Heat art the PitBoss extension uses.

**Freshness:** 500 sims per module per track · ranking data generated 2026-10-24T21:23:28Z (2026-10-24 CT) · source HeatAI `data/garage_draft/track_module_one_card_rankings.json` @ `e03ccea` · exported from HeatAI master @ `b0639ea`

These tables update only when HeatAI regenerates this export.

Non-base tracks are not listed. They are scored live in the panel, and there is no static table for them.

Advisory only. PitBoss is not affiliated with or endorsed by Days of Wonder or Board Game Arena. A simulation is not a guarantee of how a module will finish in your game.

The same rows are in [garage-by-track.csv]({{ '/assets/data/garage-by-track.csv' | relative_url }}) (500 sims each), and card definitions are in [garage-modules.csv]({{ '/assets/data/garage-modules.csv' | relative_url }}). How the panel uses ranks day to day is in the [FAQ]({{ '/faq/' | relative_url }}#how-are-garage-modules-ranked).
